<analysis>
The seed payload uses:
HTML sink: `<details>` tag with `ontoggle` event handler
JavaScript execution: IIFE wrapping `atob('YWxlcnQ=')` which decodes to 'alert', then calls `top[s](17)` → `top.alert(17)`
Active rules targeting:
: Unix command injection patterns (false positive on `top[s]`)
: PHP variable function calls `$var()` and array/string syntax
: Node.js patterns including `constructor`, function chains, and process references
: libinjection XSS detection
: Event handler pattern `on[a-z]{3,50}` with `=`
: JavaScript globals like `top` with bracket/paren access
Key tokens to mutate:
Event handler `ontoggle` (941120 target)
`top` global variable (941370, 934100)
Function call patterns (933210, 934100)
`constructor` alternative paths (934100)
Mutation approaches:
Change event handler name/structure
Replace `top` with alternative window references
Use constructor chains instead of direct calls
Fragment execution via template literals or alternative encoding
</analysis>
